import { readFile, readdir, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';

export function mergeSettings(defaults, overrides) {
  if (!overrides || typeof overrides !== 'object' || Array.isArray(overrides)) return overrides ?? defaults;
  const merged = { ...defaults };
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(overrides)) {
    merged[key] = value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
      ? mergeSettings(defaults?.[key] && typeof defaults[key] === 'object' ? defaults[key] : {}, value)
      : value;
  }
  return merged;
}

export function parseGenerationInterval(value) {
  if (value === -1 || value === '-1') return null;
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new Error('generationInterval must be -1 or duration such as "1h"');

  const match = /^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)(ms|s|m|h)$/.exec(value.trim());
  if (!match) throw new Error('generationInterval must be -1 or duration such as "1h"');

  const amount = Number(match[1]);
  const multiplier = { ms: 1, s: 1000, m: 60_000, h: 3_600_000 }[match[2]];
  const milliseconds = amount * multiplier;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(milliseconds) || milliseconds < 1) {
    throw new Error('generationInterval must resolve to a positive whole number of milliseconds');
  }
  return milliseconds;
}

export async function loadSceneSequence(scenePath, defaultGeneration, {
  scheduleEnabled = true,
  generationInterval = undefined,
} = {}) {
  const absoluteScenePath = resolve(scenePath);
  const sceneLocation = await stat(absoluteScenePath);
  const isSceneDirectory = sceneLocation.isDirectory();
  const directoryEntries = isSceneDirectory ? await readdir(absoluteScenePath, { withFileTypes: true }) : [];
  const metadataPath = isSceneDirectory ? resolve(absoluteScenePath, 'scene.json') : absoluteScenePath;
  const hasMetadataFile = isSceneDirectory && directoryEntries.some((entry) => entry.isFile() && entry.name === 'scene.json');
  const scene = isSceneDirectory
    ? hasMetadataFile ? JSON.parse(await readFile(metadataPath, 'utf8')) : {}
    : JSON.parse(await readFile(absoluteScenePath, 'utf8'));
  if (!scene || typeof scene !== 'object' || Array.isArray(scene)) {
    throw new Error('Scene JSON must contain an object');
  }
  let sceneIterations = scene.iterations;
  if (isSceneDirectory) {
    const iterationFileNames = directoryEntries
      .filter((entry) => entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith('.scene.json'))
      .map((entry) => entry.name)
      .sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right, 'en'));
    sceneIterations = await Promise.all(iterationFileNames.map(async (fileName) =>
      JSON.parse(await readFile(resolve(absoluteScenePath, fileName), 'utf8'))));
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(sceneIterations) || sceneIterations.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(isSceneDirectory
      ? `Scene folder must contain .scene.json files: ${absoluteScenePath}`
      : 'Scene JSON iterations must be a non-empty ordered list');
  }
  const minutes = scene.generationIntervalInMinutes;
  if (scheduleEnabled && generationInterval === undefined && minutes !== undefined
    && String(minutes) !== '-1' && !/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(String(minutes))) {
    throw new Error('generationIntervalInMinutes must be a positive number of minutes or -1');
  }
  const selectedInterval = generationInterval ?? (minutes === undefined ? undefined : String(minutes) === '-1' ? '-1' : `${minutes}m`);
  const activeSchedule = scheduleEnabled && selectedInterval !== '-1';
  if (activeSchedule && !['on', 'off'].includes(scene.biasMode)) {
    throw new Error('Scene JSON biasMode must be "on" or "off"');
  }

  const intervalMs = activeSchedule
    ? parseGenerationInterval(selectedInterval, sceneIterations.length)
    : null;
  const iterators = [];
  for (const [index, iteration] of sceneIterations.entries()) {
    if (!iteration || typeof iteration !== 'object' || Array.isArray(iteration)) {
      throw new Error(`Scene JSON iterations[${index}] must be an object`);
    }
    if (typeof iteration.name !== 'string' || !iteration.name.trim()) {
      throw new Error(`Scene JSON iterations[${index}].name must be a non-empty string`);
    }
    const generationOverrides = iteration.generationOverrides ?? {};
    if (!generationOverrides || typeof generationOverrides !== 'object' || Array.isArray(generationOverrides)) {
      throw new Error(`Scene JSON iterations[${index}].generationOverrides must be an object`);
    }
    iterators.push({
      name: iteration.name.trim(),
      generation: mergeSettings(defaultGeneration, generationOverrides),
    });
  }

  return {
    path: absoluteScenePath,
    scheduleEnabled: activeSchedule,
    generationInterval: activeSchedule ? selectedInterval : null,
    intervalMs,
    biasMode: activeSchedule ? scene.biasMode : null,
    iterators,
  };
}

export function getIteratorStartTime(startedAt, index, intervalMs, iteratorCount, biasMode) {
  const slotMs = intervalMs / iteratorCount;
  const biasMs = biasMode === 'on' ? slotMs / 2 : 0;
  const cycle = Math.floor(index / iteratorCount);
  const slot = index % iteratorCount;
  return startedAt + (cycle * intervalMs) + biasMs + (slot * slotMs);
}

Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/scene-count.mjs
import { loadTaktmuster } from './taktmuster.mjs';

export function resolveSceneCountSettings(options) {
  const mode = options['scene-count-mode'] || 'fixed';
  if (!['fixed', 'taktmuster'].includes(mode)) throw new Error('--scene-count-mode must be fixed or taktmuster');
  if (mode === 'fixed') {
    const count = Number(options.scenes ?? 3);
    if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 1 || count > 12) throw new Error('Use --scenes=1 through 12');
    return { mode, count };
  }
  const settings = {
    mode,
    takt: Number(options['scene-count-takt'] ?? 2),
    numerator: Number(options['scene-count-numerator'] ?? 3),
    denominator: Number(options['scene-count-denominator'] ?? 4),
    type: options['scene-count-type'] || 'balanced',
    bias: Number(options['scene-count-bias'] ?? 2),
    minimum: Number(options['min-scenes'] ?? 1),
    maximum: Number(options['max-scenes'] ?? 12),
  };
  if (!Number.isInteger(settings.takt) || settings.takt < 1 || settings.takt > 64
    || !Number.isInteger(settings.numerator) || settings.numerator < 1 || settings.numerator > 32
    || !Number.isInteger(settings.denominator) || settings.denominator < 1 || settings.denominator > 32
    || !['balanced', 'classic'].includes(settings.type) || !Number.isSafeInteger(settings.bias)
    || !Number.isInteger(settings.minimum) || !Number.isInteger(settings.maximum)
    || settings.minimum < 1 || settings.maximum > 12 || settings.maximum < settings.minimum) {
    throw new Error('Invalid scene-count rhythm: positive measures/meter, balanced|classic, integer bias and 1 <= min-scenes <= max-scenes <= 12 required');
  }
  return settings;
}

export function createSceneCountStream(settings, TaktmusterClass) {
  settings = resolveSceneCountSettings({
    'scene-count-mode': settings.mode, scenes: settings.count,
    'scene-count-takt': settings.takt, 'scene-count-numerator': settings.numerator,
    'scene-count-denominator': settings.denominator, 'scene-count-type': settings.type,
    'scene-count-bias': settings.bias, 'min-scenes': settings.minimum, 'max-scenes': settings.maximum,
  });
  let stream;
  let index = 0;
  if (settings.mode === 'taktmuster') {
    const Taktmuster = TaktmusterClass || loadTaktmuster();
    stream = new Taktmuster();
    stream.setTakt(settings.takt, settings.numerator, settings.denominator);
    stream.setType(settings.type);
  }
  return {
    settings,
    next() {
      const iteration = index++;
      if (settings.mode === 'fixed') return { iteration, count: settings.count, settings };
      const step = stream.getNext();
      const rawValue = Number(step?.patternValue ?? step);
      if (!Number.isFinite(rawValue) || rawValue <= 0) throw new Error('Taktmuster returned an invalid scene-count value');
      const biasedValue = Math.round(rawValue) + settings.bias;
      return {
        iteration, rawValue, biasedValue,
        count: Math.max(settings.minimum, Math.min(settings.maximum, biasedValue)),
        settings,
      };
    },
  };
}

    ${direction.videoMode === 'first_last_frame' ? `<dt>First-Last-Frame-Vorgaben</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(direction.lastFrame.compositionRequirements.join(' · '))}<br>Visuell konsistent halten: ${escapeHtml(direction.lastFrame.consistencyElements.join(' · '))}<br>Künftiger Bildbrief: ${escapeHtml(direction.lastFrame.generationBrief)}<br>Endbildpfad: ${escapeHtml(direction.lastFrame.imagePath)}</dd>` : ''}
    <dt>GPT-Bewegungsentwurf (ohne Kamera/Audio)</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(direction.videoPrompt)}</dd>
    ${audio ? `<dt>Audioanweisung</dt><dd>${audio.intentionalSilence ? `Stille · ${escapeHtml(audio.silenceReason)}` : `${escapeHtml(audio.ambientTexture)}${audio.soundEvents.length ? `<ul>${audio.soundEvents.map((event) => `<li>${escapeHtml(event.startSeconds)}–${escapeHtml(event.endSeconds)} s · ${escapeHtml(event.description)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : '<br>Keine einzelnen Soundereignisse'}`}<br>Von vorher: ${escapeHtml(audio.continuityFromPrevious)}<br>Zu nächster: ${escapeHtml(audio.continuityToNext)}<br>Dialog: nein</dd>` : ''}
    <dt>Alter Timing-Prompt (unverändert)</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(direction.legacyVideoPrompt)}</dd>
    </dl><p><b>Gesamtrhythmus:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.overallDirection?.movementRhythm)} · <b>Spannung:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.overallDirection?.tensionCurve)} · <b>Schluss:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.overallDirection?.endingStrategy)}</p>
    <p><b>Kamera über die Iteration:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.overallDirection?.cameraOverview)}</p>
    ${visionBlock}</section>${transitionGuide(record, sceneIndex)}`;
};
const videoIterationGuide = (record, prefix = '') => {
  const direction = record.videoDirection;
  const audio = record.audioDirection?.audioDirection || (direction?.audioDirectionEnabled ? direction.audioDirection : null);
  if (!direction && !audio) return '';
  return `<section class="video-guide"><h3>Regie über die ganze Iteration</h3>${direction
    ? `<p><b>Kamera:</b> ${escapeHtml(direction.overallDirection?.cameraOverview)} · maximaler Zoom ${escapeHtml((direction.maxZoom ?? 0.08) * 100)}%</p>`
    : '<p>Visuelle Video-Direction aus; der ursprüngliche Szenenprompt bleibt bestehen.</p>'}${audio
    ? `<p><b>Audiokonzept (${record.audioDirection?.model || direction?.model || 'GPT'}):</b> ${escapeHtml(audio.overallConcept)}<br><b>Wiederkehrende Motive:</b> ${escapeHtml(audio.recurringMotifs.join(' · ') || 'keine')}<br><b>Stimme:</b> keine Dialoge oder Erzählstimme · <b>Kontinuität:</b> ${escapeHtml(audio.continuityStrategy)} · <b>Musik:</b> ${escapeHtml(audio.musicPolicy)}<br><a href="${escapeHtml(`${prefix}audio-direction.json`)}">Audio-Regieprotokoll</a></p>`
    : '<p>Audio-Regie aus; es wird kein Audioabschnitt an den Videoprompt angehängt.</p>'}</section>`;
};
const visualControls = (scene, fallback) => {
  const options = scene.visualStyleOptions || fallback;
  if (!options) return '';
  return `<p><b>Bildparameter:</b> Symbolik ${options.symbolic ? 'AN' : 'AUS'} · Raumtiefe ${options.roomDeepness ? 'AN' : 'AUS'} · Layering ${escapeHtml(options.layering ?? 0)} / 7${scene.visualControlReason ? ` · ${escapeHtml(scene.visualControlReason)}` : ''}</p>`;
};
const semanticTrace = (record) => {
  if (!record.plan) return '';
  const entries = record.semantic?.entries || [];
  const used = entries.filter((entry) => record.plan.semanticUse?.[entry.id]?.relevance === 'relevant');
  return `<div class="semantic-trace"><h3>Stream-Einfluss · ${used.length}/${entries.length} Fragmente</h3>${used.map((entry) => {
    const use = record.plan.semanticUse[entry.id];
    return `<p><b>${escapeHtml(entry.item.title)}</b> · Szenen ${escapeHtml(use.relatedSceneIndices.join(', ') || 'Keine Zuordnung')}<br>Bildmotiv laut GPT: <b>${escapeHtml(use.visualMotif)}</b><br>${escapeHtml(use.visibleConsequence)}</p>`;
  }).join('')}<p>Freie Assoziationen laut GPT · keine inhaltliche oder wörtliche Prüfung.</p><p><b>Fiktionsgrenze:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.plan.fictionBoundary)}</p></div>`;
};
const countSelection = (record) => {
  const choice = record.sceneCountSelection;
  if (!choice) return '';
  const settings = choice.settings;
  return settings.mode === 'taktmuster'
    ? `<p><b>Szenenzahl durch Taktmuster:</b> ${escapeHtml(settings.takt)} × ${escapeHtml(settings.numerator)}/${escapeHtml(settings.denominator)} · ${escapeHtml(settings.type)} · Rohwert ${escapeHtml(choice.rawValue)} + Bias ${escapeHtml(settings.bias)} → ${escapeHtml(choice.count)} Szenen · Grenzen ${escapeHtml(settings.minimum)}–${escapeHtml(settings.maximum)}</p>`
    : `<p><b>Szenenzahl:</b> fest ${escapeHtml(choice.count)}</p>`;
};
const sceneTimingSummary = (record) => {
  const scenes = record.plan?.scenes || [];
  const knownDuration = (scene) => Number.isFinite(scene.durationSeconds) && scene.durationSeconds > 0;
  return {
    count: scenes.length || `${record.sceneCount ?? 0} geplant`,
    durations: scenes.length ? scenes.map((scene, index) => `S${index + 1}: ${knownDuration(scene) ? `${scene.durationSeconds} s` : 'offen'}`).join(' · ') : 'Noch offen',
    total: scenes.length && scenes.every(knownDuration) ? `${scenes.reduce((sum, scene) => sum + scene.durationSeconds, 0)} s` : 'Noch offen',
  };
};
const timingSummaryStyles = '.timing-summary{margin:24px 0;padding:20px;background:#252623;border:1px solid #47473e;border-radius:12px;overflow-x:auto}.timing-summary h2{margin:0 0 14px}.timing-summary table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:15px}.timing-summary th,.timing-summary td{text-align:left;vertical-align:top;padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #47473e}.timing-summary th{color:#dfc987}.timing-summary th:first-child,.timing-summary td:first-child,.timing-summary td:last-child{white-space:nowrap}';
const renderTimingSummary = (iterations, linkIterations = false) => `<section class="timing-summary"><h2>Szenenübersicht</h2><table><thead><tr><th>Iteration</th><th>Szenen</th><th>Szenenlängen</th><th>Gesamtdauer</th></tr></thead><tbody>${iterations.map(({ record }, index) => {
  const summary = sceneTimingSummary(record);
  const label = linkIterations ? `<a href="#iteration-${index + 1}">Iteration ${index + 1}</a>` : 'Diese Iteration';
  return `<tr><td>${label}</td><td>${escapeHtml(summary.count)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(summary.durations)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(summary.total)}</td></tr>`;
}).join('')}</tbody></table></section>`;
const renderMergedFilms = (iterations) => {
  const films = iterations.filter(({ record }) => record.titleCardFilm?.path || record.concatenatedVideo?.path);
  if (!films.length) return '';
  return `<section class="merged-films"><h2>Zusammengesetzte Filme · ${films.length}</h2><div class="merged-film-grid">${films.map(({ directory, record }, index) => {
    const film = record.titleCardFilm?.path
      ? { ...record.concatenatedVideo, ...record.titleCardFilm }
      : record.concatenatedVideo;
    const iterationNumber = Number(directory.match(/iteration-(\d+)/)?.[1]) || index + 1;
    const path = film.runPath || `${directory}/${film.path}`;
    return `<article class="merged-film"><h3>Iteration ${iterationNumber}${record.titleCardFilm?.path ? ' · mit Titeltafel' : ''} · MP4 ${Number(film.durationSeconds).toFixed(2)} s</h3><p>${escapeHtml(record.article?.title || '')}</p><p>Angeforderte Szenensumme: ${escapeHtml(film.requestedDurationSeconds ?? film.durationSeconds)} s</p><video controls preload="metadata" playsinline src="${escapeHtml(path)}" aria-label="Gesamtfilm Iteration ${iterationNumber}"></video>${film.gaps?.length ? `<p class="film-gap">Lücke: ${film.gaps.map((gap) => `Szene ${gap.sceneIndex}, ${gap.durationSeconds} s schwarz`).join('; ')}</p>` : ''}</article>`;
  }).join('')}</div></section>`;
};

export function renderReport(record) {
  const { article, semantic, plan, results = [], catalog = [] } = record;
  const imageReferenceExplanation = record.guernicaFigureReference === false
    ? 'GPT nutzt Artikeltext und Nachrichtenfoto für Planung und Szeneninhalt. Die Bild-API erhält ausschließlich das Nachrichtenfoto als Bildreferenz; Guernica-Vollbild und Figuren-Crops werden nicht übergeben.'
    : `GPT nutzt Artikeltext und Nachrichtenfoto für Fakten und Szenenplanung. Die Bild-API erhält die gewählte maskierte Figurenreferenz als Protagonistenreferenz${record.styleReference ? ' und das vollständige Gemälde ausschließlich als Stilreferenz' : '; ein vollständiges Gemälde wird nicht übergeben'}${record.articleImageReference ? ' sowie das Nachrichtenfoto als Referenz für die grobe visuelle Orientierung' : '; das Nachrichtenfoto wird nicht an die Bild-API übergeben'}.`;
  const costs = results.map((item) => item.cost).filter((cost) => typeof cost === 'number');
  const displayFilm = record.titleCardFilm?.path
    ? { ...record.concatenatedVideo, ...record.titleCardFilm }
    : record.concatenatedVideo;
  const scenes = (plan?.scenes || []).map((scene, index) => {
    const figure = catalog.find((item) => item.id === scene.guernicaId);
    const result = results[index];
    const filmScene = record.filmPlan?.scenePlan?.[index];
    return `<section><h2>${scene.index}. ${escapeHtml(scene.title)}</h2>
      <p><b>Hauptfigur:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.plan?.protagonist?.name || 'Noch nicht festgelegt')} · ${escapeHtml(scene.protagonistAction || scene.storyBeat)}</p>
      <p>${escapeHtml(scene.storyBeat)}</p><p>${escapeHtml(scene.durationSeconds)} s geplant · ${escapeHtml(scene.composition)}</p>
      ${visualControls(scene, record.visualStyleOptions)}
      ${result?.path ? photo(result.path, 'API-generiertes Startbild') : '<p>Startbild noch nicht gespeichert.</p>'}
      ${record.videoResults?.[index]?.path ? `<video controls preload="metadata" playsinline src="${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].path)}"></video><p>API-generiertes Video · angefordert ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].requestedDurationSeconds ?? filmScene?.durationSeconds)} s · MP4 ${Number(record.videoResults[index].durationSeconds).toFixed(2)} s · ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].resolution)} · ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].model)} · Clip-Kosten ${money(record.videoResults[index].cost)} · Renderzeit ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].renderSeconds)} s · Download ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].downloadSeconds)} s · Gesamt ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[index].totalSeconds)} s · ${audioSummary(record.videoResults[index].audioStream)}</p>` : ''}
      ${figure ? `<p><b>Figurenreferenz:</b> ${escapeHtml(figure.name)} · ${escapeHtml(scene.selectionReason)}</p><div class="reference">${photo(figure.path, 'Figurenreferenz für die Hauptfigur')}</div>` : ''}
      ${scene.crocodileAppearance ? `<p><b>Krokodil:</b> ${scene.crocodileAppearance.present ? `${escapeHtml(scene.crocodileAppearance.role)} · ${escapeHtml(scene.crocodileAppearance.placement)}` : 'Weggelassen; passt hier nicht als Metapher.'}</p>` : ''}
      ${details(`Unveränderter GPT-Bildprompt → ${record.imageModel || 'Bild-API'}`, scene.stillPrompt)}
      ${filmScene?.videoPrompt ? videoGuide(scene, filmScene, record, index) : ''}
      ${record.audioDirection?.scenes?.[index] ? `<p><b>Audio-Regie:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.audioDirection.scenes[index].sceneAudio.intentionalSilence ? `Stille · ${record.audioDirection.scenes[index].sceneAudio.silenceReason}` : `${record.audioDirection.scenes[index].sceneAudio.ambientTexture} · ${record.audioDirection.scenes[index].sceneAudio.soundEvents.map((event) => `${event.startSeconds}–${event.endSeconds} s: ${event.description}`).join(' · ') || 'keine einzelnen Soundereignisse'}`)}</p>` : ''}
      ${videoDirectionGuide(record, index)}
      ${record.sceneTiming?.scenes?.[index] ? `<p><b>Dauerbegründung:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.sceneTiming.scenes[index].reason)}</p>` : ''}
      ${result ? details('Bild-API · Kosten, Seed, Prompt-Hash', result) : ''}</section>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<!doctype html><html lang="de"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
  <title>EU · automatische Bildkomposition</title><style>
  ${timingSummaryStyles}
  *{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;background:#181918;color:#e5e1d7;font:17px/1.6 system-ui}main{max-width:1120px;margin:auto;padding:30px 20px}h1,h2{line-height:1.2}section{background:#252623;border:1px solid #47473e;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;margin:24px 0}img{display:block;max-width:100%;height:auto;margin:auto}figure{margin:16px 0}figcaption{color:#bbb7ab;font-size:14px}pre{white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:14px}summary{cursor:pointer;color:#dfc987}details{margin:16px 0}.reference{max-width:230px}.reference-gallery{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(120px,1fr));gap:12px}.reference-option{margin:0;padding:10px;border:1px solid #47473e;border-radius:8px}.reference-option img{width:100%;height:90px;object-fit:contain}.reference-option.selected{border-color:#dfc987}.reference-option figcaption{font-size:12px}.video-guide{margin:20px 0;padding:16px;border:1px solid #47473e;border-radius:8px;background:#20211f}.video-guide h3{margin:0 0 10px}.video-guide dl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(120px,180px) 1fr;gap:8px;margin:0}.video-guide dt{color:#dfc987}.video-guide dd{margin:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.video-guide a{overflow-wrap:anywhere}a{color:#dfc987}.error{color:#ffa292}.grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:24px}@media(max-width:700px){.grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.video-guide dl{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:2px}.video-guide dd{margin-bottom:8px}}
  video{display:block;width:100%;max-width:100%;height:auto;margin:16px auto;border-radius:8px;background:#111}
  </style></head><body><main><h1>Artikel + Semantic Stream → Szenenbild und Filmplan</h1>
  <p>${escapeHtml(record.status)} · ${escapeHtml(record.createdAt)} · ${escapeHtml(record.imageModel)}</p>
  ${record.generationSettings?.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt ? '<p><b>Regieprompt:</b> archivierter Guernica-Stilreferenz-Prompt vom 23.10.2026 · feste visuelle Steuerungen · P-Video-Stil-Lock aus</p>' : ''}
  ${record.generationSettings?.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePromptRequested && !record.generationSettings?.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt ? '<p><b>Regieprompt:</b> angefordert, aber inaktiv · Guernica wurde nicht als Stilreferenz gesendet</p>' : ''}
  ${renderTimingSummary([{ record }])}
  ${record.titleCard?.imagePath ? photo(record.titleCard.imagePath, 'Generierte Titeltafel aus record.json → article.title') : ''}
  ${record.titleCardFilm?.status === 'render_failed' ? `<p class="error">Titeltafel-Film fehlgeschlagen: ${escapeHtml(record.titleCardFilm.error)} · Szenenfilm bleibt verfügbar.</p>` : ''}
  ${displayFilm?.path ? `<section><h2>${record.titleCardFilm?.path ? 'Film mit Titeltafel' : 'Zusammengesetzter Film'}</h2><video controls preload="metadata" playsinline src="${escapeHtml(displayFilm.path)}"></video><p>${escapeHtml(displayFilm.generatedVideos ?? displayFilm.scenes)} Videos · ${escapeHtml(displayFilm.scenes)} Szenen · MP4 ${Number(displayFilm.durationSeconds).toFixed(2)} s · angeforderte Szenensumme ${escapeHtml(displayFilm.requestedDurationSeconds ?? displayFilm.durationSeconds)} s${displayFilm.gaps?.length ? ` · Lücke: ${displayFilm.gaps.map((gap) => `Szene ${gap.sceneIndex}, ${gap.durationSeconds} s schwarz`).map(escapeHtml).join('; ')}` : ''} · ${escapeHtml(displayFilm.method)} · ${audioSummary(displayFilm.audioStream)}</p></section>` : ''}
  ${countSelection(record)}
  ${record.sceneVisualControls ? '<p>Bildparameter werden pro Szene aus Handlung und Raum gewählt.</p>' : record.visualStyleOptions ? `<p>Feste Bildparameter für alle Szenen: Symbolik ${record.visualStyleOptions.symbolic ? 'AN' : 'AUS'} · Raumtiefe ${record.visualStyleOptions.roomDeepness ? 'AN' : 'AUS'} · Layering ${escapeHtml(record.visualStyleOptions.layering ?? 0)} / 7</p>` : ''}
  ${record.sceneDurationSettings ? `<p>Szenenlänge: ${escapeHtml(record.sceneDurationSettings.provider)} · Werte ${escapeHtml(record.sceneDurationSettings.patternValues?.join(', ') || '–')} · × ${escapeHtml(record.sceneDurationSettings.multiplier)} + Bias ${escapeHtml(record.sceneDurationSettings.bias)} · ${escapeHtml(record.sceneDurationSettings.minimum)}–${escapeHtml(record.sceneDurationSettings.maximum)} s</p>` : ''}
  ${record.planningVersion === 2 ? '<p>Bildplanung und Zeitwahl getrennt: Der fertige Bildplan bleibt bei der Dauer- und Videoplanung unverändert.</p>' : ''}
  ${record.variant ? `<p><b>${escapeHtml(record.variant.label)}</b> · ${escapeHtml(record.variant.description)}</p>` : ''}
  <p>${record.imageProvider === 'hf-flux-klein' ? 'Hugging Face ZeroGPU: GPU-Zeit wird vom Tageskontingent oder Guthaben abgezogen; Space meldet keine GPU-Sekunden.' : `Gemeldete Bildkosten: $${costs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0).toFixed(5)}`} · GPT separat: ${escapeHtml(record.usage?.total_tokens ?? '–')} Tokens</p>
  ${record.filmPlan ? `<p>${escapeHtml(record.filmPlan.status)}</p>` : ''}
  ${record.videoDirection ? `<p>Optionale Videoregie: ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.status)} · ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.model)} · Video-Modus ${escapeHtml(record.videoDirection.selectedMode)} · kein Video-Modell oder Video-Provider festgelegt.</p>` : ''}
  ${videoIterationGuide(record)}
  ${record.error ? `<p class="error">${escapeHtml(record.error)}</p>` : ''}
  ${record.visualReview ? `<section><h2>Bildprüfung</h2><p>${escapeHtml(record.visualReview.summary)}</p><ul>${record.visualReview.scenes.map((item) => `<li><b>Szene ${item.index}:</b> ${escapeHtml(item.assessment)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></section>` : ''}
  <section><h2>Quellartikel</h2><h3>${escapeHtml(article.title)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(article.publish_date)} · ${escapeHtml(article.source_country)}</p>
  ${/^https?:\/\//i.test(article.url || '') ? `<a href="${escapeHtml(article.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Quelle öffnen</a>` : ''}
  ${record.articleImage ? photo(record.articleImage.path, 'Originales Artikelbild') : ''}
  ${record.styleReference ? photo(record.styleReference.path, 'Guernica · vollständige Stilreferenz') : ''}
  ${record.guernicaFigureReference === false ? '<p><b>Bildreferenz:</b> nur Presse-/Artikelbild · kein Guernica-Vollbild · keine Guernica-Figuren-Crops</p>' : ''}
  ${details('Artikeltext', article.text || article.summary)}${details('Zufallsauswahl', record.selection)}</section>
  <section><h2>Wörter und gezogene Stream-Fragmente</h2>${details('Vollständiger Semantic-Stream-Snapshot', semantic)}
  ${(semantic?.entries || []).map((entry) => `<p><b>${escapeHtml(entry.seed.join(' · '))} → ${escapeHtml(entry.item.title)}</b></p>`).join('')}
  ${semanticTrace(record)}
  ${details('GPT: relevante Begriffe, Szenen und wörtliche Bildmotive', plan?.semanticUse || [])}</section>
  ${plan ? `<section><h2>Automatisch entwickelte Geschichte</h2><p>${escapeHtml(plan.storyPremise)}</p>${storyDramaturgy(plan)}<h3>Hauptfigur</h3><p><b>${escapeHtml(plan.protagonist?.name || 'Noch nicht festgelegt')}</b> · ${escapeHtml(plan.protagonist?.role || '')}</p><p>${escapeHtml(plan.protagonist?.visualIdentity || '')}</p><h3>Bildbeobachtung</h3><p>${escapeHtml(plan.visionAnalysis)}</p><h3>Fakten laut Artikel</h3><ul>${plan.articleFacts.map((fact) => `<li>${escapeHtml(fact)}</li>`).join('')}</ul><p><b>Fiktionsgrenze:</b> ${escapeHtml(plan.fictionBoundary)}</p>${referenceGallery(catalog, plan.protagonist?.guernicaId, '', record.guernicaFigureReference !== false)}</section>` : ''}
  ${scenes}<section><h2>Nachvollziehbarkeit</h2><p>${escapeHtml(record.variant?.promptOrigin || record.promptOrigin || imageReferenceExplanation)}</p>
  ${record.sourceVersion ? `<p>Generator-Commit: ${escapeHtml(record.sourceVersion.commit)} · Branch: ${escapeHtml(record.sourceVersion.branch || 'detached HEAD')} · ${record.sourceVersion.clean ? 'Code unverändert beim Start' : 'Lokale Codeänderungen beim Start'}</p>` : ''}
  <p>Versionierter Regieprompt: ${escapeHtml(record.directorHash || 'noch nicht geladen')}</p>
  ${record.visualPlanHash ? `<p>Fixierter Bildplan: <a href="visual-plan.json">Bildplan vor Zeitwahl</a> · SHA-256 ${escapeHtml(record.visualPlanHash)}${record.sceneTiming ? ' · <a href="timing-request.json">Zeitplanung: Eingabe</a> · <a href="timing-response.json">Zeitplanung: Antwort</a>' : ''}</p>` : ''}
  <p><a href="record.json">Laufprotokoll</a> · ${record.autoMode ? '<a href="auto-decisions.json">Auto-Regieentscheidung</a> · ' : ''}<a href="semantic-stream.json">Stream-Snapshot</a> · <a href="scene-plan.json">Szenenplan</a> · ${record.plan?.storyArc ? '<a href="story-arc.json">Story Arc</a> · ' : ''}${record.videoVision ? '<a href="video-vision.json">Vision-Analyse</a> · ' : ''}${record.videoDirection ? '<a href="video-direction.json">Video Direction</a> · ' : ''}${record.filmPlan ? '<a href="film-plan.json">Film-Übergabeplan</a> · ' : ''}${record.filmPlan ? '<a href="film-agent-prompt.md">Anweisung für den Film-Generator</a> · ' : ''}<a href="gpt-request.json">GPT-Eingabe</a> · <a href="gpt-response.json">GPT-Antwort</a> · <a href="director.md">Regieprompt</a></p>
  ${(plan?.scenes || []).map((scene) => `<p>Szene ${scene.index}: <a href="scene-${scene.index}-request.json">Bild-Request</a> · <a href="scene-${scene.index}-response.json">Bild-Response</a></p>`).join('')}
  ${details('Vollständiges Laufprotokoll', record)}</section></main></body></html>`;
}

export function renderRunReport(run, iterations) {
  const totalVideoCost = iterations.reduce((sum, { record }) => sum + (record.videoResults || []).reduce((sceneSum, video) => sceneSum + (typeof video?.cost === 'number' ? video.cost : 0), 0), 0);
  const totalImageCost = iterations.reduce((sum, { record }) => sum + (record.results || []).reduce((sceneSum, image) => sceneSum + (typeof image?.cost === 'number' ? image.cost : 0), 0), 0);
  const reports = iterations.map(({ directory, record }, iterationIndex) => {
    const prefix = `${directory}/`;
    const displayFilm = record.titleCardFilm?.path
      ? { ...record.concatenatedVideo, ...record.titleCardFilm }
      : record.concatenatedVideo;
    const scenes = (record.plan?.scenes || []).map((scene, sceneIndex) => {
      const result = record.results?.[sceneIndex];
      const filmScene = record.filmPlan?.scenePlan?.[sceneIndex];
      return `<article class="scene"><h3>Szene ${scene.index}: ${escapeHtml(scene.title)}</h3>
        <p><b>Hauptfigur:</b> ${escapeHtml(record.plan?.protagonist?.name || 'Noch nicht festgelegt')} · ${escapeHtml(scene.protagonistAction || scene.storyBeat)}</p>
        <p>${escapeHtml(scene.storyBeat)} · ${escapeHtml(scene.durationSeconds)} s</p>
        ${visualControls(scene, record.visualStyleOptions)}
        ${result?.path ? photo(`${prefix}${result.path}`, 'API-generiertes Startbild') : '<p>Startbild noch nicht gespeichert.</p>'}
        ${record.videoResults?.[sceneIndex]?.path ? `<video controls preload="metadata" playsinline src="${escapeHtml(`${prefix}${record.videoResults[sceneIndex].path}`)}"></video><p>API-generiertes Video · angefordert ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].requestedDurationSeconds ?? filmScene?.durationSeconds)} s · MP4 ${Number(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].durationSeconds).toFixed(2)} s · ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].resolution)} · ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].model)} · Clip-Kosten ${money(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].cost)} · Renderzeit ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].renderSeconds)} s · Download ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].downloadSeconds)} s · Gesamt ${escapeHtml(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].totalSeconds)} s · ${audioSummary(record.videoResults[sceneIndex].audioStream)}</p>` : ''}
        ${filmScene?.videoPrompt ? videoGuide(scene, filmScene, record, sceneIndex, prefix) : ''}

import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { basename, extname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { hash } from './planner.mjs';

async function loadSharp() {
  try {
    return (await import('sharp')).default;
  } catch (error) {
    // Worktrees can use the dependency installation already configured for their stream.
    const modulePath = process.env.NEWS_SEMANTIC_STREAM_MODULE;
    if (error.code !== 'ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND' || !modulePath) throw error;
    return createRequire(resolve(modulePath))('sharp');
  }
}

export async function prepareImageReference(directory, input) {
  const buffer = await readFile(join(directory, input.path));
  if (hash(buffer) !== input.sha256) throw new Error(`Reference changed: ${input.path}`);
  const sharp = await loadSharp();
  const oriented = await sharp(buffer).rotate().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true });
  const { width, height } = oriented.info;
  if (width >= 128 && height >= 128 && width <= 2048 && height <= 2048) return input;

  // Runware requires both dimensions in [128, 2048]. Preserve the full composition.
  const scale = Math.min(2048 / Math.max(width, height), Math.max(1, 128 / Math.min(width, height)));
  const targetWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * scale));
  const targetHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(height * scale));
  const paddingX = Math.max(0, 128 - targetWidth);
  const paddingY = Math.max(0, 128 - targetHeight);
  const output = await sharp(oriented.data)
    .resize(targetWidth, targetHeight)
    .extend({
      left: Math.floor(paddingX / 2), right: Math.ceil(paddingX / 2),
      top: Math.floor(paddingY / 2), bottom: Math.ceil(paddingY / 2),
      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },
    })
    .png().toBuffer();
  const stem = basename(input.path, extname(input.path));
  const path = `media/${stem}-api.png`;
  await writeFile(join(directory, path), output);
  return {
    ...input, path, sourcePath: input.path, sourceSha256: input.sha256,
    mimeType: 'image/png', sha256: hash(output), bytes: output.length,
    width: targetWidth + paddingX, height: targetHeight + paddingY,
    dataUrl: `data:image/png;base64,${output.toString('base64')}`,
  };
}

-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--
Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/provider-resilience.mjs
const retryableNetworkCodes = new Set([
  'ECONNRESET',
  'ECONNREFUSED',
  'ETIMEDOUT',
  'EAI_AGAIN',
  'ENOTFOUND',
  'UND_ERR_CONNECT_TIMEOUT',
  'UND_ERR_HEADERS_TIMEOUT',
  'UND_ERR_SOCKET',
]);

export function isRetryableNetworkError(error) {
  if (error?.name === 'AbortError' || error?.name === 'TimeoutError') return true;
  if (error?.name !== 'TypeError' && error?.name !== 'FetchError') return false;
  let cause = error.cause;
  while (cause) {
    if (retryableNetworkCodes.has(cause.code)) return true;
    cause = cause.cause;
  }
  return error.message === 'fetch failed';
}

export function isRetryableHttpStatus(status) {
  return status === 408 || status === 425 || status === 429 || status >= 500;
}

export function retryDelayMs(attempt, baseDelayMs = 1000, maxDelayMs = 15000) {
  const exponentialDelay = Math.min(maxDelayMs, baseDelayMs * (2 ** (attempt - 1)));
  return Math.min(maxDelayMs, Math.round(exponentialDelay * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4)));
}

export async function retryTransient(operation, {
  attempts = 5,
  baseDelayMs = 1000,
  maxDelayMs = 15000,
  shouldRetry = isRetryableNetworkError,
  onRetry = () => {},
  wait = (milliseconds) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds)),
} = {}) {
  for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      return await operation(attempt);
    } catch (error) {
      if (attempt >= attempts || !shouldRetry(error)) throw error;
      const delay = retryDelayMs(attempt, baseDelayMs, maxDelayMs);
      onRetry({ attempt, nextAttempt: attempt + 1, delay, error });
      await wait(delay);
    }
  }
}

const containsUntranslatedStreamTerm = (text, entry) => {
  const [word, language] = entry.seed || [];
  if (!word || language === 'en' || word.length < 4 || /^[A-Z]{2,}$/.test(word)) return false;
  return new RegExp(`(^|[^\\p{L}\\p{N}])${escapeRegex(word)}([^\\p{L}\\p{N}]|$)`, 'iu').test(text);
};

    { type: 'text', text: `Required scene count: exactly ${sceneCount}. ${continuationInstruction} Follow the visual controls embedded in the director instructions and JSON input.` },
    { type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(data) },
    { type: 'text', text: `SOURCE CODE CONTEXT (concatenated file sections):\n${compositionSourceContext}` },
  ];
  for (const image of images) {
    content.push({ type: 'text', text: image.label });
    content.push({ type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: image.dataUrl, detail: ['article', 'style', 'location'].includes(image.role) ? 'high' : 'low' } });
  }
  const schema = structuredClone(planSchema);
  if (!figureReferenceProvided) {
    schema.properties.protagonist.properties.guernicaId = { type: 'integer', enum: [0] };
    schema.properties.scenes.items.properties.guernicaId = { type: 'integer', enum: [0] };
  }
  schema.properties.scenes.items.properties.index.maximum = sceneCount;
  schema.properties.scenes.minItems = sceneCount;
  schema.properties.scenes.maxItems = sceneCount;
  if (sceneVisualControls) {
    const properties = schema.properties.scenes.items.properties;
    schema.properties.scenes.items.required.push('visualStyleOptions', 'visualControlReason');
    properties.visualStyleOptions = object({
      symbolic: { type: 'boolean' },
      roomDeepness: { type: 'boolean' },
      layering: { type: 'integer', minimum: 0, maximum: 7 },
    });
    properties.visualControlReason = { type: 'string', minLength: 20, maxLength: 1000 };
  }
  if (storyClou) {
    const sceneProperties = schema.properties.scenes.items.properties;
    schema.properties.scenes.items.required.push('dramaticFunction');
    sceneProperties.dramaticFunction = {
      type: 'string',
      enum: ['setup', 'development', 'escalation', 'conflict', 'turning_point', 'revelation', 'resolution', 'final_clou'],
    };
    schema.properties.storyArc = object({
      setup: string,
      protagonistGoal: string,
      centralContradiction: string,
      development: string,
      turningPoint: string,
      finalClou: string,
      clouType: string,
      clouExplanation: string,
      foreshadowing: list(object({ sceneIndex: { type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: sceneCount }, element: string, function: string })),
    });
    schema.properties.clouReveal = object({
      narrativeClou: string,
      intendedVisualChange: string,
      startImageMayContainClou: { type: 'boolean' },
      motionRevealPossible: { type: 'boolean' },
      requiredFinalState: string,
      explanation: string,
    });
    schema.required.push('storyArc', 'clouReveal');
  }
  const relevance = { type: 'string', enum: ['relevant', 'not_relevant'], description: 'Creative use in this fictional story, not factual relevance to the news article.' };
  schema.properties.semanticUse = object(Object.fromEntries(semantic.entries.map((entry) => [entry.id, object({
    relevance, relatedSceneIndices: list({ type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: sceneCount }), visualMotif: string, visibleConsequence: string,
  })])));
  const reasoningModel = /^gpt-6(?:\.1-sol|-luna)(?:-|$)/.test(model);
  const reasoningEffort = model.startsWith('gpt-6-luna') ? 'medium' : 'high';
  const request = {
    model, messages: [{ role: 'system', content: director }, { role: 'user', content }],
    response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: { name: 'eu_news_scene_plan', strict: true, schema } },
    ...(reasoningModel ? { reasoning_effort: reasoningEffort } : { temperature: 0.65 }),
    max_completion_tokens: Math.max(14000, sceneCount * 2000),
  };
  const log = structuredClone(request);
  let index = 0;
  for (const part of log.messages[1].content) {
    if (part.type === 'image_url') part.image_url.url = `local:${images[index++].path}`;
  }
  return { request, log, director, directorHash: hash(director) };
}

export function validatePlan(response, { sceneCount, catalog, semantic, continuationPlan = null, visualStyleOptions = null, sceneDurationSettings = null, visualOnly = false, sceneVisualControls = false, storyClou = false, figureReferenceProvided = true }) {
  const choice = response.choices?.[0];
  if (choice?.message?.refusal) throw new Error(`GPT refusal: ${choice.message.refusal}`);
  if (choice?.finish_reason !== 'stop') throw new Error('GPT did not return a complete plan');
  const plan = JSON.parse(choice.message.content);
  if (!Array.isArray(plan.scenes) || plan.scenes.length !== sceneCount) throw new Error('Incorrect scene count');
  if (storyClou) {
    const arc = plan.storyArc;
    const reveal = plan.clouReveal;
    const arcTextFields = ['setup', 'protagonistGoal', 'centralContradiction', 'development', 'turningPoint', 'finalClou', 'clouType', 'clouExplanation'];
    if (!arc || arcTextFields.some((field) => typeof arc[field] !== 'string' || !arc[field].trim())
      || !Array.isArray(arc.foreshadowing)
      || !reveal || ['narrativeClou', 'intendedVisualChange', 'requiredFinalState', 'explanation'].some((field) => typeof reveal[field] !== 'string' || !reveal[field].trim())
      || typeof reveal.startImageMayContainClou !== 'boolean' || typeof reveal.motionRevealPossible !== 'boolean') {
      throw new Error('Invalid story arc or clou reveal structure');
    }
    for (const setup of arc.foreshadowing) {
      if (!setup || !Number.isInteger(setup.sceneIndex) || setup.sceneIndex < 1 || setup.sceneIndex > sceneCount
        || typeof setup.element !== 'string' || !setup.element.trim()
        || typeof setup.function !== 'string' || !setup.function.trim()) {
        throw new Error('Invalid story foreshadowing scene reference');
      }
    }
    if (plan.scenes.at(-1)?.dramaticFunction !== 'final_clou') {
      throw new Error('The final scene must carry the final_clou dramatic function');
    }
    if (plan.scenes.some((scene) => !['setup', 'development', 'escalation', 'conflict', 'turning_point', 'revelation', 'resolution', 'final_clou'].includes(scene.dramaticFunction))) {
      throw new Error('Every scene needs a valid dramatic function');
    }
  }
  const figureIds = new Set(catalog.map((item) => item.id));
  const validProtagonistReference = figureReferenceProvided
    ? figureIds.has(plan.protagonist?.guernicaId)
    : plan.protagonist?.guernicaId === 0;
  if (!plan.protagonist?.name?.trim() || !plan.protagonist.role?.trim() || !plan.protagonist.visualIdentity?.trim() || !validProtagonistReference) {
    throw new Error(figureReferenceProvided
      ? 'The story needs one named protagonist with a valid Guernica character reference'
      : 'The story needs one named protagonist and guernicaId 0 when no figure reference is supplied');
  }
  if (continuationPlan && (plan.protagonist.name !== continuationPlan.protagonist?.name || plan.protagonist.guernicaId !== continuationPlan.protagonist?.guernicaId)) {
    throw new Error('An expanded plan must preserve the original protagonist and figure reference');
  }
  for (const [index, scene] of plan.scenes.entries()) {
    const protagonistName = plan.protagonist.name.toLocaleLowerCase();
    const protagonistGivenName = protagonistName.split(/\s+/)[0];
    const protagonistAction = scene.protagonistAction?.toLocaleLowerCase() || '';
    const namesProtagonist = protagonistAction.includes(protagonistName)
      || protagonistAction.includes(protagonistGivenName);
    if (scene.index !== index + 1) throw new Error(`Scene ${index + 1} must use its matching scene index`);
    if (figureReferenceProvided ? !figureIds.has(scene.guernicaId) : scene.guernicaId !== 0) {
      throw new Error(figureReferenceProvided
        ? `Scene ${scene.index} must select a Guernica figure reference from the supplied catalog`
        : `Scene ${scene.index} must use guernicaId 0 because no figure reference is supplied`);
    }
    if (!namesProtagonist) throw new Error(`Scene ${scene.index} protagonistAction must repeat the protagonist name "${plan.protagonist.name}" instead of using a pronoun or omission`);
    if (!scene.selectionReason?.trim()) throw new Error(`Scene ${scene.index} must explain why its selected figure reference fits the action`);
    if (!planSchema.properties.scenes.items.properties.composition.enum.includes(scene.composition)) throw new Error('Each scene needs a supported composition');
    if (sceneVisualControls) {
      const controls = scene.visualStyleOptions;
      if (!controls || typeof controls.symbolic !== 'boolean' || typeof controls.roomDeepness !== 'boolean' || !Number.isInteger(controls.layering) || controls.layering < 0 || controls.layering > 7 || typeof scene.visualControlReason !== 'string' || scene.visualControlReason.trim().length < 20) {
        throw new Error('Every scene needs its own valid visual controls and a reason for choosing them');
      }
    }
    if (visualOnly) {
      if (Object.hasOwn(scene, 'durationSeconds') || Object.hasOwn(scene, 'videoPrompt')) throw new Error('Visual planning must not assign timing or video prompts');
    } else {
      const minimumSceneSeconds = sceneDurationSettings?.minimum ?? 5;
      const maximumSceneSeconds = sceneDurationSettings?.maximum ?? 15;
      if (!Number.isInteger(scene.durationSeconds) || scene.durationSeconds < minimumSceneSeconds || scene.durationSeconds > maximumSceneSeconds) throw new Error('Invalid scene duration');
      if (typeof scene.videoPrompt !== 'string' || scene.videoPrompt.length < 80 || scene.videoPrompt.length > 5000) throw new Error('Invalid scene video prompt');
    }
    if (typeof scene.stillPrompt !== 'string' || scene.stillPrompt.length < 200 || scene.stillPrompt.length > 10000) throw new Error('Invalid still prompt length');
    for (const streamEntry of semantic.entries) {
      if (containsUntranslatedStreamTerm(scene.stillPrompt, streamEntry) || (!visualOnly && containsUntranslatedStreamTerm(scene.videoPrompt, streamEntry))) {
        throw new Error('Image and video prompts must be English; translate semantic-stream terms instead of copying source-language words');
      }
    }
  }
  const expected = semantic.entries.map((entry) => entry.id).sort();
  const actual = Object.keys(plan.semanticUse || {}).sort();
  if (JSON.stringify(expected) !== JSON.stringify(actual)) throw new Error('GPT must assess each semantic entry exactly once');
  // Validate structure only. GPT owns the creative connection and wording.
  for (const entry of Object.values(plan.semanticUse)) {
    if (!entry || !['relevant', 'not_relevant'].includes(entry.relevance)
      || !Array.isArray(entry.relatedSceneIndices) || typeof entry.visualMotif !== 'string'
      || typeof entry.visibleConsequence !== 'string'
      || entry.relatedSceneIndices.some((index) => !Number.isInteger(index) || index < 1 || index > sceneCount)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid semantic metadata format or scene index');
    }
  }
  return plan;
}

export function buildImageTask(scene, taskUUID, referenceImages) {
  if (referenceImages.length > 3) throw new Error('At most three reference images are supported');
  return {
    taskType: 'imageInference', taskUUID, model: imageModel,
    positivePrompt: scene.stillPrompt,
    width: 1680, height: 944, numberResults: 1,
    outputType: 'URL', outputFormat: 'JPG', includeCost: true,
    ...(referenceImages.length ? { inputs: { referenceImages } } : {}),
  };
}

Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/news-ranking.mjs
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFile, readdir, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { articleKey } from '../scripts/news-eu-relevance.mjs';

const readJson = async (path) => JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
const rankedCollectionCache = new Map();

async function getCollectionFileVersion(newsDirectory, directoryEntries) {
  const relevantFiles = directoryEntries
    .filter((name) => /^all-.*\.json$/.test(name)
      || (/^eu-relevance-.*\.json$/.test(name) && !name.endsWith('-checkpoint.json')))
    .sort();
  const versions = await Promise.all(relevantFiles.map(async (name) => {
    try {
      const fileStats = await stat(join(newsDirectory, name));
      return `${name}:${fileStats.mtimeMs}:${fileStats.size}:${fileStats.ino}`;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
      throw error;
    }
  }));
  return versions.filter(Boolean).join('|');
}

export function relevanceFileName(aggregate, aggregateFile) {
  const sourceSpecificName = `eu-relevance-${aggregateFile.replace(/^all-/, '').replace(/\.json$/, '')}.json`;
  const dateTag = String(aggregate.date || aggregateFile).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9-]/g, '-');
  return [sourceSpecificName, `eu-relevance-${dateTag}.json`];
}

export async function findRankedNewsCollections(newsDirectory) {
  let directoryEntries;
  try {
    directoryEntries = await readdir(newsDirectory);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return [];
    throw error;
  }
  const version = await getCollectionFileVersion(newsDirectory, directoryEntries);
  const cached = rankedCollectionCache.get(newsDirectory);
  if (cached?.version === version) return cached.collections;

  const files = directoryEntries
    .filter((name) => /^all-.*\.json$/.test(name))
    .sort()
    .reverse();
  const rankedCollections = [];

  for (const aggregateFile of files) {
    const aggregatePath = join(newsDirectory, aggregateFile);
    let aggregate;
    let aggregateHash;
    try {
      const contents = await readFile(aggregatePath);
      aggregate = JSON.parse(contents.toString('utf8'));
      aggregateHash = createHash('sha256').update(contents).digest('hex');
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === 'ENOENT') continue;
      throw error;
    }
    let relevance = null;
    let relevancePath = null;
    for (const fileName of relevanceFileName(aggregate, aggregateFile)) {
      const candidatePath = join(newsDirectory, fileName);
      try {
        relevance = await readJson(candidatePath);
        relevancePath = candidatePath;
        break;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
      }
    }
    if (!relevance) continue;
    if (relevance.sourceFile !== aggregateFile || !Object.keys(relevance.entries || {}).length) continue;

    const scores = new Map(Object.entries(relevance.entries).map(([key, entry]) => {
      const score = Number(entry.euRelevance);
      return [key, Number.isFinite(score) ? score : undefined];
    }));
    const seenStoryImages = new Set();
    const candidates = (aggregate.news || [])
      .map((article, index) => ({ article, aggregateIndex: index, score: scores.get(articleKey(article)) }))
      .filter(({ article }) => /^https?:\/\//i.test(article.image || '') && (article.text || article.summary))
      .sort((left, right) => (right.score ?? -1) - (left.score ?? -1)
        || (right.article.publish_date ?? '').localeCompare(left.article.publish_date ?? '')
        || left.aggregateIndex - right.aggregateIndex)
      .filter(({ article }) => {
        let imageUrl;
        try {
          imageUrl = new URL(article.image);
          imageUrl.hash = '';
        } catch {
          return false;
        }
        const storyImage = [article.source_country || '', article.publish_date || '', imageUrl.href].join('|');
        if (seenStoryImages.has(storyImage)) return false;
        seenStoryImages.add(storyImage);
        return true;
      });

    if (candidates.length) rankedCollections.push({
      aggregate,
      aggregateFile,
      aggregatePath,
      aggregateHash,
      relevance,
      relevancePath,
      candidates,
      fetchedAt: aggregate.fetchedAt || null,
    });
  }

  rankedCollectionCache.set(newsDirectory, { version, collections: rankedCollections });
  return rankedCollections;
}

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFile, writeFile, rename, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join, basename } from 'node:path';

const SPACE_URL = 'https://black-forest-labs-flux-3-image-klein.hf.space';
const OUTPUT_SETTINGS = {
  mode_choice: 'Distilled (16 steps)',
  randomize_seed: false,
  width: 1680,
  height: 944,
  num_inference_steps: 16,
  guidance_scale: 1,
  prompt_upsampling: true,
};

const readJson = async (path) => JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
async function writeJson(path, value) {
  const temporaryPath = `${path}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(temporaryPath, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`);
  await rename(temporaryPath, path);
}

async function request(path, token, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(`${SPACE_URL}${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, ...options.headers },
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(240_000),
  });
  const body = await response.text();
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Hugging Face FLUX Space HTTP ${response.status}: ${body.slice(0, 1000)}`);
  return body;
}

async function uploadImage(imagePath, token) {
  const buffer = await readFile(imagePath);
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append('files', new Blob([buffer]), basename(imagePath));
  const response = JSON.parse(await request('/gradio_api/upload', token, { method: 'POST', body: form }));
  if (!Array.isArray(response) || typeof response[0] !== 'string') throw new Error('Hugging Face image upload returned no file path');
  return { image: { path: response[0], meta: { _type: 'gradio.FileData' }, orig_name: basename(imagePath) }, caption: null };
}

async function poll(eventId, token) {
  const response = await fetch(`${SPACE_URL}/gradio_api/call/v2/generate/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`, {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(240_000),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Hugging Face image polling failed: HTTP ${response.status}`);

  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = '';
  let currentEvent = '';
  while (true) {
    const { value, done } = await reader.read();
    buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: !done });
    const lines = buffer.split(/\r?\n/);
    buffer = lines.pop() || '';
    for (const line of lines) {
      if (line.startsWith('event:')) currentEvent = line.slice(6).trim();
      if (!line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
      const data = line.slice(5).trim();
      if (currentEvent === 'error') throw new Error(`Hugging Face image generation failed: ${data}`);
      if (currentEvent === 'complete') {
        const result = JSON.parse(data);
        if (!Array.isArray(result) || !result[0]) throw new Error('Hugging Face generation returned no completed image');
        return result;
      }
    }
    if (done) break;
  }
  throw new Error('Hugging Face generation stream ended before completion');
}

export async function generateFluxKleinImage({ prompt, referencePaths, token, directory, name, seed }) {
  if (!token) throw new Error('Set HF_TOKEN to use Hugging Face ZeroGPU');
  const requestPath = join(directory, `${name}-hf-request.json`);
  const submissionPath = join(directory, `${name}-hf-submission.json`);
  const responsePath = join(directory, `${name}-hf-response.json`);
  const pendingPath = join(directory, `${name}-hf-pending.json`);
  const requestLog = {
    provider: 'huggingface-zerogpu',
    space: 'black-forest-labs/flux-3-image-klein',
    endpoint: '/generate',
    prompt,
    referencePaths: referencePaths.map((path) => path.replace(`${directory}/`, '')),
    settings: { ...OUTPUT_SETTINGS, seed },
  };
  if (!(await exists(requestPath))) await writeJson(requestPath, requestLog);
  else if ((await readJson(requestPath)).prompt !== prompt) throw new Error(`${name}: saved FLUX prompt differs from scene plan`);

  if (await exists(responsePath)) {
    const saved = await readJson(responsePath);
    if (!saved.eventId) throw new Error(`${name}: saved Hugging Face response has no event id; refusing to submit again`);
    const completed = await poll(saved.eventId, token);
    await writeJson(responsePath, { eventId: saved.eventId, data: completed, completedAt: saved.completedAt, refreshedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    await unlink(pendingPath).catch((error) => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; });
  } else {
    let eventId;
    if (await exists(submissionPath)) {
      eventId = (await readJson(submissionPath)).eventId;
      if (!eventId) throw new Error(`${name}: saved Hugging Face submission has no event id`);
    } else {
      if (await exists(pendingPath)) {
        throw new Error(`${name}: Hugging Face submission outcome is unknown; refusing to submit again and risk duplicate generation`);
      }
      const inputImages = [];
      for (const imagePath of referencePaths) inputImages.push(await uploadImage(imagePath, token));
      const body = {
        prompt,
        input_images: inputImages,
        ...OUTPUT_SETTINGS,
        seed,
      };
      await writeJson(join(directory, `${name}-hf-api-request.json`), body);
      try {
        await writeFile(pendingPath, `${JSON.stringify({ startedAt: new Date().toISOString(), provider: 'huggingface-zerogpu', status: 'submitting' }, null, 2)}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
      } catch (error) {
        if (error.code === 'EEXIST') throw new Error(`${name}: Hugging Face submission outcome is unknown; refusing to submit again and risk duplicate generation`);
        throw error;
      }
      const submitted = JSON.parse(await request('/gradio_api/call/v2/generate', token, {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body),
      }));
      if (!submitted.event_id) throw new Error(`${name}: Hugging Face returned no event id; outcome remains pending and will not be resubmitted automatically`);
      eventId = submitted.event_id;
      await writeJson(submissionPath, { eventId, submittedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    }
    const completed = await poll(eventId, token);
    await writeJson(responsePath, { eventId, data: completed, completedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    await unlink(pendingPath).catch((error) => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; });
  }

  const saved = await readJson(responsePath);
  const outputImage = saved.data?.[0];
  const imageLocation = outputImage?.url || (outputImage?.path
    ? `${SPACE_URL}/gradio_api/file=${encodeURIComponent(outputImage.path)}`
    : null);
  if (!imageLocation) throw new Error(`${name}: saved Hugging Face response has no downloadable image`);
  const imageUrl = new URL(imageLocation, SPACE_URL).toString();
  return { imageUrl, seed: saved.data?.[1] ?? seed, eventId: saved.eventId };
}

async function exists(path) {
  try { await readFile(path); return true; } catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw error; }
}

export const autoDirectorPromptVersion = 'eu-reski-auto-director-v1';

export function resolveAutoModeSetting(configuredValue = false, explicitArguments = []) {
  const enable = explicitArguments.includes('--auto-mode');
  const disable = explicitArguments.includes('--no-auto-mode');
  if (enable && disable) throw new Error('Choose either --auto-mode or --no-auto-mode');
  if (enable) return true;
  if (disable) return false;
  return configuredValue === true;
}

const flagKeys = [
  'storyClou',
  'legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt',
  'preserveCubistVideoStyle',
  'sceneVisualControls',
  'articleImageReference',
  'landscapePrompt',
  'videoDirection',
  'videoVision',
  'videoAudioDirection',
  'videoMaxZoom',
];

export const autoDirectorSystemPrompt = [
  'You are the artistic film director for the experimental EU-RESKI news-film installation.',
  'For this news article, first form a coherent film concept, then choose a unified combination of the available creative settings.',
  'Consider dramaturgy, cubist image-making, Guernica references, spatial composition, motion, camera, visual continuity, and sound.',
  'Do not activate features just because they exist. A quiet, reduced, fragmentary, cyclical, or open-ended approach can be right.',
  'The existing Story planner remains responsible for the concrete story and scenes. Existing scene-specific visual controls remain responsible for their per-scene choices.',
  'Preserve EU-RESKI’s cubist identity. Disabling the extra cubist video lock never requests photorealism. Spoken content must follow the existing no-dialogue setting.',
  'Choose only schema values. Do not change models, providers, image/video resolution, costs, iteration count, article order, scene count/timing, paths, references, or operational settings.',
  'videoVision=on requires videoDirection=on. Scene video mode is fixed to executable image_to_video.',
  'Return every flag, all four short concept fields, and a concise reason.',
].join(' ');

const flagSchema = {
  type: 'object',
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: flagKeys,
  properties: {
    storyClou: { type: 'string', enum: ['on', 'off'] },
    legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: { type: 'boolean' },
    preserveCubistVideoStyle: { type: 'boolean' },
    sceneVisualControls: { type: 'string', enum: ['scene-specific', 'fixed'] },
    articleImageReference: { type: 'boolean' },
    landscapePrompt: { type: 'boolean' },
    videoDirection: { type: 'string', enum: ['on', 'off'] },
    videoVision: { type: 'string', enum: ['on', 'off'] },
    videoAudioDirection: { type: 'string', enum: ['on', 'off'] },
    videoMaxZoom: { type: 'number', minimum: 0, maximum: 0.3 },
  },
};

export const autoDirectorSchema = {
  type: 'object',
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: ['dramaturgicalConcept', 'visualConcept', 'motionConcept', 'audioConcept', 'flags', 'reason'],
  properties: {
    dramaturgicalConcept: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 1200 },
    visualConcept: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 1200 },
    motionConcept: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 1200 },
    audioConcept: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 1200 },
    flags: flagSchema,
    reason: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 2000 },
  },
};

export function buildAutoDirectorRequest({ article, model, constraints = {} }) {
  const input = Object.fromEntries(['id', 'title', 'publish_date', 'url', 'source_country', 'language', 'summary', 'text']
    .map((key) => [key, String(article?.[key] ?? '').slice(0, key === 'text' ? 18000 : 4000)]));
  const request = {
    model,
    messages: [
      { role: 'system', content: autoDirectorSystemPrompt },
      { role: 'user', content: JSON.stringify({
        article: input,
        availableFlags: {
          storyClou: 'on | off; on activates the existing Story planner arc fields.',
          legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: 'true | false; true selects the archived director when style reference is active and Guernica location reference is off.',
          preserveCubistVideoStyle: 'true | false; true appends the existing cubist video style lock unless legacy director is effective.',
          sceneVisualControls: 'scene-specific | fixed; scene-specific reuses existing per-scene symbolic, roomDeepness, and layering decisions.',
          articleImageReference: 'true | false; controls whether the already downloaded article image is sent to image generation.',
          landscapePrompt: 'true | false; adds existing wide-frame composition guidance without changing image dimensions.',
          videoDirection: 'on | off; uses the existing per-iteration video director.',
          videoVision: 'on | off; uses existing iteration image analysis and requires videoDirection=on.',
          videoAudioDirection: 'on | off; uses existing audio planning and must respect the fixed no-dialogue setting.',
          videoMaxZoom: 'number from 0 through 0.3; limits the existing video director zoom/crop change without selecting a different model or mode.',
        },
        fixedConstraints: {
          sceneVideoMode: 'image_to_video',
          noDialogueNarration: constraints.noDialogueNarration === true,
          styleReferenceAvailable: constraints.styleReferenceAvailable === true,
          guernicaLocationReference: constraints.guernicaLocationReference === true,
          articleImageAvailable: constraints.articleImageAvailable === true,
          fixedVisualStyleOptions: constraints.fixedVisualStyleOptions || null,
          sceneCount: constraints.sceneCount ?? null,
          sceneTiming: constraints.sceneTiming || null,
          imageProvider: constraints.imageProvider || null,
          videoModel: constraints.videoModel || null,
          videoResolution: constraints.videoResolution || null,
          videoSettingsMode: constraints.videoSettingsMode || null,
          videosAfterEachIteration: constraints.videosAfterEachIteration === true,
        },
      }) },
    ],
    response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: { name: 'eu_reski_auto_director', strict: true, schema: autoDirectorSchema } },
    ...( /^gpt-6(?:\.1-sol|-luna)(?:-|$)/.test(model)
      ? { reasoning_effort: model.startsWith('gpt-6-luna') ? 'medium' : 'high' }
      : { temperature: 0.65 }),
    max_completion_tokens: 3000,
  };
  return request;
}

export function validateAutoDirectorResponse(response) {
  const choice = response?.choices?.[0];
  if (choice?.message?.refusal) throw new Error(`Auto director refusal: ${choice.message.refusal}`);
  if (choice?.finish_reason !== 'stop') throw new Error('Auto director did not return a complete decision');
  let result;
  try { result = JSON.parse(choice.message.content); } catch { throw new Error('Auto director returned invalid JSON'); }
  const allowedKeys = ['dramaturgicalConcept', 'visualConcept', 'motionConcept', 'audioConcept', 'flags', 'reason'];
  if (!result || Object.keys(result).length !== allowedKeys.length || allowedKeys.some((key) => !Object.hasOwn(result, key))) {
    throw new Error('Auto director response has missing or unknown fields');
  }
  for (const key of ['dramaturgicalConcept', 'visualConcept', 'motionConcept', 'audioConcept', 'reason']) {
    if (typeof result[key] !== 'string' || !result[key].trim()) throw new Error(`Auto director field ${key} must be non-empty text`);
  }
  const flags = result.flags;
  if (!flags || Object.keys(flags).length !== flagKeys.length || flagKeys.some((key) => !Object.hasOwn(flags, key))) {
    throw new Error('Auto director flags are incomplete or contain unknown settings');
  }
  if (!['on', 'off'].includes(flags.storyClou)
    || typeof flags.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt !== 'boolean'
    || typeof flags.preserveCubistVideoStyle !== 'boolean'
    || !['scene-specific', 'fixed'].includes(flags.sceneVisualControls)
    || typeof flags.articleImageReference !== 'boolean'
    || typeof flags.landscapePrompt !== 'boolean'
    || !['on', 'off'].includes(flags.videoDirection)
    || !['on', 'off'].includes(flags.videoVision)
    || !['on', 'off'].includes(flags.videoAudioDirection)) {
    throw new Error('Auto director selected an unsupported flag value');
  }
  if (typeof flags.videoMaxZoom !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(flags.videoMaxZoom) || flags.videoMaxZoom < 0 || flags.videoMaxZoom > 0.3) {
    throw new Error('Auto director selected an unsupported videoMaxZoom value');
  }
  if (flags.videoVision === 'on' && flags.videoDirection !== 'on') {
    throw new Error('Auto director selected videoVision=on without videoDirection=on');
  }
  return structuredClone(result);
}

export function resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(selectedFlags, constraints = {}) {
  const effectiveFlags = { ...selectedFlags };
  const constraintsApplied = [
    'Scene video mode is fixed to image_to_video because first_last_frame is not executable in the current renderer.',
    `noDialogueNarration remains user-controlled (${constraints.noDialogueNarration === true ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}).`,
  ];
  if (selectedFlags.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt
    && (!constraints.styleReferenceAvailable || constraints.guernicaLocationReference)) {
    effectiveFlags.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt = false;
    constraintsApplied.push(constraints.guernicaLocationReference
      ? 'Legacy Guernica director is ineffective while Guernica location reference is enabled.'
      : 'Legacy Guernica director is ineffective because the style reference is disabled.');
  }
  if (effectiveFlags.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt) {
    if (effectiveFlags.sceneVisualControls === 'scene-specific') {
      effectiveFlags.sceneVisualControls = 'fixed';
      constraintsApplied.push('Legacy Guernica director forces fixed visual controls.');
    }
    if (effectiveFlags.preserveCubistVideoStyle) {
      effectiveFlags.preserveCubistVideoStyle = false;
      constraintsApplied.push('Legacy Guernica director suppresses the additional Cubist Video Lock.');
    }
  }
  effectiveFlags.videoMode = 'image_to_video';
  if (effectiveFlags.videoVision === 'on' && effectiveFlags.videoDirection !== 'on') {
    throw new Error('Effective auto settings require videoVision=on to have videoDirection=on');
  }
  return { effectiveFlags, constraintsApplied };
}

export function resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, { iteration, articleId, model, promptVersion, hasDependentWork, constraints = {} }) {
  if (!saved) {
    if (hasDependentWork) throw new Error('Auto decision is missing after dependent iteration work began; refusing to choose new settings on resume');
    return null;
  }
  if (saved.autoMode !== true || saved.iteration !== iteration || saved.articleId !== articleId
    || saved.model !== model || saved.promptVersion !== promptVersion) {
    throw new Error('Saved auto decision does not match this iteration, article, model, or prompt version');
  }
  const recordedConstraints = saved.constraints || constraints;
  const constraintKeys = ['noDialogueNarration', 'styleReferenceAvailable', 'guernicaLocationReference', 'articleImageAvailable'];
  if (constraintKeys.some((key) => constraints[key] !== recordedConstraints[key])) {
    throw new Error('Saved auto decision fixed constraints changed; refusing to resume with different effective settings');
  }
  const validated = validateAutoDirectorResponse({
    choices: [{ finish_reason: 'stop', message: { content: JSON.stringify({
      dramaturgicalConcept: saved.dramaturgicalConcept,
      visualConcept: saved.visualConcept,
      motionConcept: saved.motionConcept,
      audioConcept: saved.audioConcept,
      flags: saved.selectedFlags,
      reason: saved.reason,
    }) } }],
  });
  const resolved = resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(validated.flags, recordedConstraints);
  if (JSON.stringify(resolved.effectiveFlags) !== JSON.stringify(saved.effectiveFlags)
    || JSON.stringify(resolved.constraintsApplied) !== JSON.stringify(saved.constraintsApplied)) {
    throw new Error('Saved auto decision effective settings no longer match this iteration constraints');
  }
  return saved;
}

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

const list = { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } };
const object = (properties) => ({
  type: 'object',
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: Object.keys(properties),
  properties,
});

export const audioDirectorPromptVersion = 'eu-reski-audio-director-v5';

export function audioDirectionInputHash({ record, model, noDialogueNarration = false }) {
  return createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify({
    promptVersion: audioDirectorPromptVersion,
    model,
    noDialogueNarration,
    storyPremise: record.plan.storyPremise,
    scenes: record.plan.scenes.map(({ index, title, storyBeat, protagonistAction, durationSeconds, videoPrompt }) => ({
      index, title, storyBeat, protagonistAction, durationSeconds, videoPrompt,
    })),
  })).digest('hex');
}

export function buildAudioDirectionRequest({ record, model, noDialogueNarration = false }) {
  const scenes = record.plan.scenes.map((scene) => ({
    sceneIndex: scene.index,
    title: scene.title,
    storyBeat: scene.storyBeat,
    visibleAction: scene.protagonistAction,
    durationSeconds: scene.durationSeconds,
    timingPrompt: scene.videoPrompt,
  }));
  const sceneAudio = object({
    soundEvents: {
      type: 'array',
      items: object({
        description: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
        startSeconds: { type: 'number', minimum: 0 },
        endSeconds: { type: 'number', minimum: 0 },
      }),
    },
    ambientTexture: { type: 'string' },
    continuityFromPrevious: { type: 'string' },
    continuityToNext: { type: 'string' },
    intentionalSilence: { type: 'boolean' },
    silenceReason: { type: 'string' },
    spokenKind: { type: 'string', enum: ['dialogue', 'narration', 'none'] },
    spokenText: { type: 'string' },
  });

  return {
    model,
    temperature: 0.45,
    max_completion_tokens: Math.max(3000, scenes.length * 600),
    messages: [
      { role: 'system', content: [
        'You are an audio director. Plan sound only for the supplied locked scenes.',
        'Do not rewrite or add visual actions. Do not direct camera, image, or video motion. Sound events must be plausible consequences of the described action; invent no unsupported event.',
        `Create one coherent audio identity across the iteration, with recurring motifs only when useful. ${noDialogueNarration
          ? 'No spoken dialogue, narration, or voices. Set spokenKind=none and spokenText empty in every scene.'
          : 'Spoken dialogue or narration is allowed when useful, but is not required in every scene. Use spokenKind and spokenText to describe it when present; otherwise use spokenKind=none and an empty spokenText.'} Music is optional and need not play in every scene.`,
        'For every sound event, use seconds relative to its scene. startSeconds must be before endSeconds, and both must fit the exact scene duration. Keep ambientTexture concise and concrete.',
        'Every scene must have concise, non-empty continuityFromPrevious and continuityToNext text. For the first scene, continuityFromPrevious describes how its sound begins; do not leave it empty.',
        'Intentional silence is allowed. Keep intentionalSilence, silenceReason, ambientTexture, and soundEvents as independent cues. They may coexist or conflict; do not reject or normalize such combinations. When both silence and sound are described, preserve both so the video model can interpret them in context.',
        'Audio direction is a separate prompt for the video model. It must not alter the supplied visual prompt.',
      ].join('\n') },
      { role: 'user', content: JSON.stringify({
        storyPremise: record.plan.storyPremise,
        protagonist: record.plan.protagonist?.name || null,
        scenes,
      }) },
    ],
    response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: {
      name: 'eu_news_audio_direction', strict: true,
      schema: object({
        audioDirection: object({
          overallConcept: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
          recurringMotifs: list,
          voicePolicy: { type: 'string', enum: [noDialogueNarration ? 'no_spoken_audio' : 'spoken_audio_allowed'] },
          musicPolicy: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
          continuityStrategy: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
        }),
        scenes: {
          type: 'array', minItems: scenes.length, maxItems: scenes.length,
          items: object({
            sceneIndex: { type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: scenes.length },
            sceneAudio,
          }),
        },
      }),
    } },
  };
}

export function validateAudioDirectionResponse(response, { record, noDialogueNarration = false }) {
  const choice = response.choices?.[0];
  if (choice?.message?.refusal) throw new Error(`Audio direction refusal: ${choice.message.refusal}`);
  if (choice?.finish_reason !== 'stop') throw new Error('GPT did not return complete audio direction');
  const direction = JSON.parse(choice.message.content);
  const expectedScenes = record.plan.scenes;
  const concept = direction.audioDirection;
  if (!concept?.overallConcept?.trim() || !Array.isArray(concept.recurringMotifs)
    || concept.voicePolicy !== (noDialogueNarration ? 'no_spoken_audio' : 'spoken_audio_allowed')
    || !concept.musicPolicy?.trim() || !concept.continuityStrategy?.trim()
    || !Array.isArray(direction.scenes) || direction.scenes.length !== expectedScenes.length) {
    throw new Error('Invalid whole-story audio direction');
  }
  for (const [index, { sceneAudio }] of direction.scenes.entries()) {
    const original = expectedScenes[index];
    if (direction.scenes[index].sceneIndex !== original.index || !sceneAudio
      || !['dialogue', 'narration', 'none'].includes(sceneAudio.spokenKind)
      || typeof sceneAudio.spokenText !== 'string'
      || (noDialogueNarration && (sceneAudio.spokenKind !== 'none' || sceneAudio.spokenText.trim()))
      || typeof sceneAudio.intentionalSilence !== 'boolean'
      || !Array.isArray(sceneAudio.soundEvents) || typeof sceneAudio.ambientTexture !== 'string'
      || !sceneAudio.continuityFromPrevious?.trim() || !sceneAudio.continuityToNext?.trim()
      || (sceneAudio.intentionalSilence && !sceneAudio.silenceReason?.trim())) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid audio direction for scene ${original.index}`);
    }
    for (const event of sceneAudio.soundEvents) {
      if (!event.description?.trim() || !Number.isFinite(event.startSeconds) || !Number.isFinite(event.endSeconds)
        || event.startSeconds < 0 || event.endSeconds <= event.startSeconds || event.endSeconds > original.durationSeconds) {
        throw new Error(`Audio event outside scene ${original.index} duration`);
      }
    }
  }
  return direction;
}

export function buildAudioPrompt(sceneAudio, audioDirection, noDialogueNarration = false) {
  if (!sceneAudio || !audioDirection) return '';
  const silenceInstruction = sceneAudio.intentionalSilence
    ? `Intentional silence cue: ${sceneAudio.silenceReason}`
    : '';
  const soundInstruction = `${sceneAudio.ambientTexture}${sceneAudio.soundEvents.length
    ? ` Sound events: ${sceneAudio.soundEvents.map((event) => `${event.startSeconds.toFixed(1)}–${event.endSeconds.toFixed(1)}s: ${event.description}`).join('; ')}.`
    : ' No discrete sound event.'} ${sceneAudio.continuityFromPrevious} ${sceneAudio.continuityToNext}`;
  const instruction = [silenceInstruction, soundInstruction,
    sceneAudio.intentionalSilence && sceneAudio.soundEvents.length
      ? 'Both silence and sound cues are intentional; preserve this tension and interpret it in context.'
      : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  const spokenInstruction = noDialogueNarration
    ? 'No spoken dialogue or narration.'
    : sceneAudio.spokenKind !== 'none' || sceneAudio.spokenText.trim()
      ? `Include ${sceneAudio.spokenKind} and speak exactly: "${sceneAudio.spokenText}".`
      : 'No spoken dialogue or narration in this scene.';
  return `AUDIO: Generate synchronized sound with this video. ${instruction} Overall sound identity: ${audioDirection.overallConcept}. Music policy: ${audioDirection.musicPolicy}. ${spokenInstruction}`;
}
